### [1] [A Hydrogen-Like Model for Pion and Kaon Masses Based on Dirac Quark-Hamiltonians and Numerical Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21459)
*Zhenhua Ning,A. S. Davuluru,H. Raposo,Jong-Ping Hsu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于新型广义杨-米尔斯对称性的相对论性类氢介子质量谱模型，引入四阶场方程以生成线性禁闭势，仅需单个禁闭荷 f_c 而非QCD中的三色荷；通过含两个短程禁闭势的狄拉克哈密顿量数值求解，以3个耦合常数+1个参数拟合31个π/K介子质量（140–2300 MeV），相对误差≈18%，并指出 f_c ≈ 8.87 fm 为夸克相互作用的基本长度标度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决标准QCD中色禁闭机制缺乏解析可解模型的问题，寻求一个能直接导出线性禁闭势、且避免复杂色动力学的简约相对论性介子谱学框架。

Method: 构建具有新广义Yang-Mills对称性的Lagrangian，推导出含四阶导数的强相互作用场方程；由此导出线性禁闭势；建立相对论性狄拉克哈密顿量，包含两个短程禁闭势；按同位旋、G宇称、总角动量J与宇称P分类介子子谱；采用数值本征值求解器计算质量谱。

Result: 成功拟合31个π和K介子的质量（范围140–2300 MeV），相对百分比误差≈18%；确定禁闭荷标度 f_c = 0.045/MeV ≡ 8.87 fm，作为夸克相互作用的基本长度；表明单禁闭荷 f_c 可替代SU(3)_c色荷实现有效禁闭。

Conclusion: 该模型提供了一个数学自洽、参数简洁的相对论性介子质量谱描述，其核心是新型Yang-Mills对称性与四阶场方程所诱导的线性禁闭；结果支持禁闭可由单一标量型禁闭荷 f_c 实现，暗示QCD色结构或可在低能有效理论中被更简约的对称性所替代。

Abstract: We consider a relativistic hydrogen-like model for meson mass spectra. The model is based on a Lagrangian with a new general Yang-Mills symmetry. The strong interactions involve a fourth-order field equation that generates a linear confining potential. Notably, this model for meson masses requires quarks to carry only a single `confining charge' $f_c$ rather than three color charges. We classify meson mass spectra into sub-spectra defined by isospin, G-parity, total angular momentum and parity. By applying a numerical solver to the Dirac Hamiltonians--incorporating two short-range confining potentials, we obtain energy eigenvalues in natural units. The model has three coupling constants and one parameter. It provides a robust fit of 31 pions and kaons masses (from $\approx 140 MeV$ to $\approx 2,300 MeV$) to within $\approx 18\%$ of relative percentage error. The model suggests that the confining charge $f_c$=0.045/MeV = 8.87 fm plays the role of the basic length in the quark interactions.

</details>


### [2] [$Δ$ resonance contributions to QED radiative corrections in neutron and inverse beta decay](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22845)
*Oleksandr Tomalak,Yi-Bo Yang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在重子手征微扰论框架下引入Δ(1232)共振态，系统计算其对中子衰变与反β衰变（IBD）中π介子诱导的QED辐射修正的贡献；发现Δ不重整矢量流耦合常数g_V，但显著改善轴矢流耦合常数g_A的幂次计数，并将实验与格点QCD比值g_A/(g^QCD_A g_V)的QED修正压至零附近；同时使IBD截面的π诱导QED修正增强20–30%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决在重子手征有效理论中忽略Δ(1232)共振态所导致的轴矢流辐射修正中幂次破坏（power counting violation）问题，并提升中子衰变与IBD过程QED修正的理论精度，以弥合实验测量与格点QCD计算之间的系统性偏差。

Method: 采用含显式Δ自由度的重子手征微扰论（HBChPT with explicit Δ），在pion-loop主导的QED辐射修正中纳入Δ共振态的中间态贡献，严格按手征幂次展开计算矢量流和轴矢流算符的重整化效应，并评估其对IBD微分截面及g_A/(g^QCD_A g_V)比值的影响。

Result: Δ共振态不改变矢量电荷g_V的QED辐射修正（即Z_V = 1）；对轴矢电荷g_A，Δ的引入恢复手征幂次一致性，并使g_A/(g^QCD_A g_V)的QED修正由显著非零值减小至0 ± δ（δ为辐射修正不确定性）；IBD过程的π诱导QED修正整体增大1.2–1.3倍。

Conclusion: Δ(1232)共振是中子衰变与IBD中π介子型QED辐射修正不可或缺的动力学自由度；其纳入不仅确保手征展开的自洽性，而且实质性降低g_A相关观测量的理论误差，对精确检验CKM幺正性及格点QCD标定具有关键意义。

Abstract: We incorporate the $Δ(1232)$ resonance into pion-induced QED radiative corrections to neutron decay and inverse beta decay (IBD). Within the framework of heavy-baryon chiral perturbation theory with explicit $Δ$ degrees of freedom, we compute additional contributions and study their impact on IBD cross sections and on the renormalization of the nucleon isovector vector and axial-vector charges. $Δ$ resonance does not renormalize the vector charge. For the axial-vector charge, including the $Δ$ resonance restores power counting and significantly reduces the QED radiative correction to the experiment-over-lattice-QCD ratio $g_A/\left(g^\mathrm{QCD}_A g_V\right)$, bringing it to zero within radiative-correction uncertainties. $Δ$ resonance increases the pion-induced QED radiative corrections to IBD by a factor $1.2$-$1.3$.

</details>


### [3] [Know What You Don't Flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21509)
*Anja Butter,Sascha Diefenbacher,Tilman Plehn,Lorenz Vogel*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文探讨了在LHC物理中生成式神经网络的校准不确定性建模，通过显式似然玩具模型对比异方差与贝叶斯归一化流对系统与统计不确定性的学习能力，并在无显式似然情形下利用分类器重加权训练异方差损失，最后以top pair事例验证条件异方差流对全相空间方向不确定性传播的校准能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高能物理中生成式模型需提供校准的不确定性估计，尤其在缺乏精确似然函数时，如何区分并传播系统与统计不确定性是关键挑战。

Method: 采用异方差归一化流（heteroscedastic normalizing flow）与贝叶斯归一化流，在显式似然玩具模型中学习相空间密度的不确定性；在隐式似然情形下，结合分类器重加权（classifier-reweighting）训练异方差损失；构建条件异方差流以实现全相空间方向的不确定性传播。

Result: 异方差与贝叶斯归一化流均能有效分离并建模系统与统计不确定性；在无显式似然下，分类器重加权策略使异方差损失仍可实现不确定性校准；条件异方差流在top pair事例中成功将校准不确定性传播至全部相空间维度。

Conclusion: 条件异方差归一化流是一种可行且鲁棒的不确定性校准框架，适用于LHC中基于生成模型的物理分析，尤其在似然不可得或计算昂贵的情形下具有实用价值。

Abstract: Calibrated learned uncertainties are a key requirement also for generative neural networks in LHC physics. For a toy model with an explicit likelihood we show how a heteroscedastic and a Bayesian normalizing flow learn the systematic and statistical uncertainties on the underlying phase space density. Without an explicit likelihood we train the heteroscedastic loss on a classifier-reweighted approximate generative network. We illustrate our comprehensive approach for top pair events and show how a conditional heteroscedastic flow propagates calibrated uncertainties to all phase space directions.

</details>


### [4] [Probing Glueball content of X(2370) through heavy quarkonium radiative decays and electron-positron annihilation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23428)
*Ruilin Zhu,Qiang Zhao*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文分析了赝标胶球的光锥分布振幅，指出双胶子与夸克-反夸克对贡献在量子圈图层次自然混合；基于η-η'-G-η_c四重混合方案，结合实验数据提取混合参数，并成功预测ψ(2S)→P+γ分支比；进一步发现J/ψ和ψ(2S)辐射衰变到X(2370)+γ对胶球-粲偶素混合角φ_gc高度敏感，存在特定角度下的相消/相长干涉；最后计算了赝标介子电生截面，理论与BESIII/Belle-II可检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 厘清X(2370)是否为胶球候选态，揭示其与η, η', η_c的混合机制，并通过辐射跃迁过程检验胶球-粲偶素混合动力学。

Method: 采用光锥QCD求和规则分析赝标胶球分布振幅；构建η-η'-G-η_c四重混合唯象模型；利用J/ψ→P+γ实验分支比拟合混合参数；预言ψ(2S)→P+γ及J/ψ/ψ(2S)→X(2370)+γ的分支比；计算e⁺e⁻→e⁺e⁻P（P=X(2370), η, η'）的电生微分截面。

Result: 成功复现实验测得的J/ψ→P+γ分支比并预测ψ(2S)→P+γ与实验一致；确定X(2370)辐射衰变对胶球-粲偶素混合角φ_gc极为敏感：J/ψ→X(2370)+γ在φ_gc≈0.01处相消，在φ_gc>0.02时转为相长；ψ(2S)→X(2370)+γ相消点位于φ_gc≈0.11；电生截面理论值与实验同量级，给出X(2370)电生截面预言。

Conclusion: X(2370)具有显著胶球-粲偶素混合成分，其辐射跃迁行为可作为胶球本征性质的关键探针；当前BESIII与Belle-II实验具备精确测量能力，有望确认其胶球本质。

Abstract: In this paper, we first analyze the light-cone distribution amplitudes of the pseudoscalar Glueball and point out that the two-gluon and quark-antiquark pair contributions naturally mix at the quantum loop level. Based on the $η-η'-G-η_c$ tetra-mixing scheme and existing experimental results, we analyze the branching ratio of $J/ψ\to P+γ$ with pseudoscalar meson $P$ and extract the mixing parameters of the tetra-mixing scheme. We then successfully predict the branching ratio of $ψ(2S) \to P+γ$, which is found to be in good agreement with experimental results. We also find that the branching ratios of $J/ψ\to X(2370)+γ$ and $ψ(2S) \to X(2370)+γ$ are highly sensitive to the Glueball-charmonium mixing angle: the destructive mixing interference in the branching ratio of $J/ψ\to X(2370)+γ$ occurs at $φ_{gc}\approx0.01$, whereas that in $ψ(2S) \to X(2370)+γ$ occurs at $φ_{gc}\approx 0.11$. Moreover, the $J/ψ\to X(2370)+γ$ branching ratio exhibits constructive mixing interference when the mixing angle is larger than $φ_{gc}\approx 0.02$. Finally, we also study the electroproduction cross sections of pseudoscalar mesons. After excluding the resonance contributions, the theoretical calculations agree with the experimental measurements in order of magnitude, and we also present theoretical predictions for the electroproduction cross section of the $X(2370)$. These theoretical results can be readily tested by current BESIII and Belle-II experiments, and further experimental measurements will reveal the intrinsic nature of the $X(2370)$.

</details>


### [5] [Electroweak and Single Top-Quark Conspiracy in Current LHC Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21510)
*Christoph Englert,Roman Kogler,Michael Spannowsky*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过SMEFT框架分析LHC上单顶夸克与tH产生道的偏离，指出弱顶偶极矩与三规范玻色子耦合变形是主导效应，并探讨了能生成该算符组合的最小新物理模型——多阈值矢量样夸克扇区。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释LHC观测到的电弱单顶夸克及tH产生率偏离，而多玻色子过程仍符合标准模型的现象；利用规范对称性将偏离归因于共同紫外起源。

Method: 采用标准模型有效场论（SMEFT）联合拟合LHC电弱单顶、tH产生截面与Z极点可观测量；进行算符重要性排序与拟合优度检验；系统扫描弱耦合可重整匹配下各类新粒子多重态（标量、矢量、矢量样夸克）生成目标算符的能力。

Result: 确定主导偏离的SMEFT算符为：弱顶偶极项（cφQ）、三规范耦合项（cW, cB）及正号左手顶流项（cQq^(3)）；纯三规范项置零时仍可接受拟合；单一SU(2)L多重态无法复现全部算符；最小实现需含singlet、doublet与两个triplet的矢量样夸克扇区，其中电流项树图生成，偶极项需额外圈图机制。

Conclusion: 观测到的LHC电弱顶夸克产生异常最可能源于多阈值矢量样夸克扇区；未来需协同提升单顶、tH/ttH测量精度，并加强VLQ直接搜寻与味物理约束。

Abstract: Recent LHC measurements of electroweak single top-quark and top-quark associated Higgs boson production show a number of rate shifts, while related multiboson channels remain close to their Standard Model predictions. Gauge symmetry relates these processes such that deviations can be organised as the low-energy imprint of a common ultraviolet origin. A Standard Model Effective Field Theory (SMEFT) analysis of these rates together with $Z$-pole observables identifies the pattern: a weak top dipole and a triple-gauge deformation carry the largest pulls, accompanied by a positive left-handed top-quark current. An acceptable fit is also obtained with the pure triple-gauge coefficient set to zero. We then ask whether a gauge-invariant model can produce this set of operators and still pass the numerous constraints from experimental data. Within weakly coupled renormalisable matching, no single scalar, vector or vector-like-quark multiplet is sufficient to reproduce the full pattern. A minimal possibility is a multi-threshold vector-like-quark sector (a singlet, a doublet and two triplets) whose currents arise at tree level, while the dipole requires additional loop dynamics that the current sector does not fix. A representative realisation, confronted with direct, indirect and flavour constraints, points to correlated measurements of electroweak single top-quark production, improved $tH$ and $t \bar t H$ sensitivity, and updated searches for vector-like quarks.

</details>


### [6] [Disentangling Charged-Current Scalar NSI from the Solar Sector at JUNO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21521)
*Adriano Cherchiglia,Pietro Chimenti,Christiane Frigerio Martins,Guilherme A. Nogueira,Pedro Pasquini,Orlando L. G. Peres*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在量子场论框架下，利用反应堆反中微子约束带电流标量非标准相互作用（NSI），推导了包含标量NSI贡献的电子反中微子存活概率至O(ε⁴)阶解析表达式，指出其引起能谱畸变并允许大θ₁₂解；基于JUNO初步数据首次实现反应堆实验中NSI参数的单味分辨测量，并证明一年数据可使太阳区分析在3σ置信水平上解除NSI与θ₁₂的简并。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决反应堆中微子实验中NSI参数与标准振荡参数（尤其是θ₁₂）之间的强简并问题，并实现对带电流标量NSI的严格、模型无关约束。

Method: 在量子场论框架下构建含标量NSI的中微子振荡哈密顿量，解析展开存活概率至O(ε⁴)，结合JUNO能谱数据进行似然拟合与参数估计，采用单味NSI参数化而非传统味和形式。

Result: 首次从反应堆实验获得单味分辨的NSI参数限制（εₑₑ, εₑₜ, εₑₛ）；证明一年JUNO数据可在3σ水平解除εₑₑ–θ₁₂简并；发现标量NSI可兼容大θ₁₂解并导致特征性能谱畸变。

Conclusion: 带电流标量NSI可通过高统计反应堆实验（如JUNO）以单味精度探测，其效应不可忽略且具独特能谱指纹，为超越标准模型的中微子物理提供了新探针。

Abstract: We constrain charged-current scalar non-standard interaction with reactor antineutrinos within a quantum field theory framework of neutrino oscillations. We derive an analytical expression up to $\mathcal{O}(\varepsilon^{4})$ for the electron antineutrino survival probability including scalar NSI contributions, and show that these effects induce spectral distortions that admit a large mixing angle solution for $θ_{12}$. Using the 59.1-day and preliminary 207.2-day JUNO datasets, we obtain the first neutrino-oscillation determination of NSI parameters individually, rather than in a flavor-summed combination, from reactor experiments. Assuming one year of data taken, we show that the only solar sector analysis breaks the degeneracy with the solar mixing angle at $3σ$ of C.L.

</details>


### [7] [Photon production from gluon splitting and fusion induced by a magnetic field of arbitrary strength in relativistic heavy-ion collisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21600)
*Alejandro Ayala,Santiago Bernal-Langarica,José Jorge Medina-Serna,Ana Julia Mizher*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文计算了半中心相对论重离子碰撞预平衡阶段，在磁场存在下由胶子融合与劈裂过程产生的光子产额和椭圆流系数，结果与PHENIX实验数据高度吻合，尤其成功再现了v2的形状与大小，为解决‘光子谜题’提供了关键进展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决高能重离子碰撞中直接光子椭圆流（v2）长期存在的理论-实验偏差问题，即‘光子谜题’——传统热辐射模型无法解释观测到的强v2信号。

Method: 基于预平衡glasma态的玻色-爱因斯坦占据因子及束流方向快速膨胀导致的各向异性胶子分布，结合任意强度外加磁场下的胶子融合（g+g→γ+g）与磁致劈裂（g→γ+q+q̄）过程，采用动理学框架计算光子产额与v2。

Result: 在glasma型与各向异性两种预平衡胶子分布假设下，计算所得光子v2的幅值与pT依赖关系均与PHENIX实验数据高度一致；同时光子总产额亦符合实验约束。

Conclusion: 预平衡阶段强磁场诱导的非热光子产生机制（尤其是磁致胶子劈裂）是解释直接光子强椭圆流的关键物理源，表明光子辐射主要发生于QGP热化之前，从而为光子作为早期时序探针提供了坚实理论基础。

Abstract: We compute the yield and elliptic flow coefficient for photons produced during the pre-equilibrium stage of semi-central relativistic heavy-ion collisions from the gluon fusion and splitting processes induced by the presence of a magnetic field. The calculation is performed for arbitrary values of the field strength. The pre-equilibrium gluon distribution is modeled with a glasma-inspired Bose-Einstein occupation factor, as well as with an anisotropic distribution that accounts for the rapid initial expansion along the beam axis. In both cases, we find a very good agreement between the calculation and the experimental data from PHENIX. Most notably, the shape and strength of the elliptic flow coefficient are described quite well, representing a step towards solving the outstanding photon puzzle.

</details>


### [8] [Textures of dimension-six operators in the SMEFT with non-invertible selection rules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21707)
*Tatsuo Kobayashi,Hajime Otsuka,Morimitsu Tanimoto,Kei Yamamoto*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了在非可逆选择规则约束下，标准模型有效场论（SMEFT）中维度六算符的味结构，系统分类了所有重子数守恒的维度六算符的味纹理，导出了其Wilson系数的解析约束形式，并指出其味结构未必与Yukawa耦合对齐，从而给出区别于最小味破坏假设及常规味对称性的B介子物理与带电流轻子味破坏辐射衰变的特征预言。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 突破最小味破坏（MFV）假设和传统味对称性框架，探究更普适的、由非可逆选择规则所决定的有效算符味结构及其可观测效应。

Method: 基于非可逆选择规则对SMEFT中所有重子数守恒的维度六算符进行系统性味纹理分类，解析推导Wilson系数的张量结构与独立参数个数，并对比MFV与常规味对称性下的味-手征模式。

Result: 获得所有baryon-number-conserving dim-6 SMEFT算符的味纹理分类；Wilson系数被约束为含特定零元与张量结构的解析表达式，且其独立参数数目显著减少；发现高维算符味结构可偏离Yukawa耦合方向，导致新型味-手征模式。

Conclusion: 非可逆选择规则诱导出区别于MFV和常规味对称性的新型味结构，为B介子衰变与ℓi→ℓjγ等过程提供可检验的特征信号，拓展了SMEFT中味物理的理论可能性。

Abstract: We investigate the flavor structures of dimension-six operators in the Standard Model Effective Field Theory (SMEFT) subject to non-invertible selection rules. Inertial motion is blind to the scale of the form and to the torsion the propagator defines, and that blindness is a geometric precursor of the \emph{universality of free fall}, before gravity enters. Newton's Second Law is a compatibility between two derivatives along a curve. The force exceeds the mass times the lowered acceleration by an exact term, and the law survives arbitrary torsion. Requiring force-free motion to be inertial along curves parametrised by arc length restricts the torsion to be totally antisymmetric. The remaining torsion is a three-form which we postulate the particle carries along its worldline. Its contraction with the velocity annihilates that velocity, so the supplementary condition of spinning-particle mechanics holds identically, and we propose that contraction as a classical spin. The algebra is familiar from relativistic spin hydrodynamics; the assignment, to the best of our knowledge, has not been proposed elsewhere. Since inertial motion resolves neither the mass nor the spin, the predictive content lies in the transport law we postulate, which turns the spin against a parallel-transported frame at a rate fixed by the acceleration alone and by no coupling constant. That rate becomes a laboratory number only once the arc-length parameter is identified with a clock, which we leave open. The dynamics asks non-degeneracy of the form, so this spacetime is not Newton--Cartan's Galilean one, and gravitation lies outside the construction. We prove every mathematical result, make three constitutive postulates and one physical conjecture.

Abstract: Many imaging problems seek to reconstruct underlying configurations from partial observable measurements. While reconstruction algorithms operate on these measurements, the observable organization induced by the measurement process is rarely represented explicitly.
  We introduce TENSKEL, a combinatorial observable framework for structured measurement and reconstruction based on tensor representations defined over discrete domains. Starting from a binary latent ensemble, the framework constructs a hierarchy of tensors coupling measurement contexts to a latent Pascal organization through successive aggregation and folding operations. Each measurement context induces an observable partition of the same latent ensemble, and the resulting tensor formulation makes explicit the associated combinatorial multiplicities, shell organization, degeneracies, and induced reconstruction geometry.
  Rather than introducing a new reconstruction algorithm, this framework provides a mathematical representation of how latent configurations become organized under observation. The induced tensor kernel characterizes similarities between latent coordinates through their measurement-context responses, while regularized inversion provides a structured reconstruction of the latent representation from observable measurements. The binary construction further admits a natural multinomial extension to discrete simplex-supported latent representations.
  Connections to Pascal cellular automata and structured discrete color mappings illustrate respectively compressed and multinomial realizations of the framework. Abstract: This book is a self-contained, tutorial introduction to topology, differential geometry, and general relativity for students and researchers in physics. Its distinguishing feature is a Mathematica-based approach that makes abstract constructions concrete through explicit, runnable notebooks and worked examples. A key contribution is a large collection of original Mathematica notebooks, written by the authors themselves, that let readers run, verify, and extend every demonstration. The first part covers point-set topology, topological spaces, continuity of maps, homotopy, and the fundamental group with applications that highlight the role of topology in modern physics. The second and largest part develops differential geometry from the ground up: manifolds, tangent and dual spaces, vector fields, pullbacks and pushforwards, Lie brackets and Lie algebras, local flows, and the Lie derivative. It then treats tensors, differential forms, the exterior derivative, volume forms, the metric tensor, and Hodge duality, emphasizing coordinate-free formulations and their computational realization. These tools are applied to Maxwell's equations in the language of forms and the generalized Stokes theorem, while Lie groups, fiber bundles, connections, and curvature bridge geometry and gauge-theoretic physics. The final part uses this framework to present general relativity, computing curvature tensors and field equations for standard spacetimes with dedicated Mathematica packages. Throughout, the book balances mathematical rigor with hands-on computation, enabling readers both to understand the theory and to reproduce every result themselves.

Result: 证明了在满足符号定（sign-definite）且空间局域的原子密度扰动下，系统必然产生至少一个束缚态；并在多个精确可解模型中显式计算了束缚态能量、波函数及依赖于扰动参数的阈值行为。

Conclusion: 束缚态的存在性本质上由扰动的符号与局域性决定，而非具体形状；该结论为设计基于人工原子阵列的拓扑光子束缚态或量子记忆单元提供了普适理论依据。

Abstract: We study a one-dimensional scalar model for an effective-mass quasiparticle field coupled to a continuum of two-level atoms with at most one excitation present. We prove a general theorem on the existence of bound states under sign-definite spatially localized perturbations of constant atomic density and analyze the bound states for several exactly solvable examples.

</details>

### [55] [Calculation of neutron electric dipole moment from Lattice QCD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22587)
*Thomas Blum,Fangcheng He,Taku Izubuchi,Luchang Jin,Hiroshi Ohki,Sergey Syritsyn*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文通过格点QCD方法，在背景电场下利用Feynman-Hellmann定理，从核子基态间局域拓扑荷密度矩阵元出发，非微扰地确定中子电偶极矩dn对θ角的依赖关系；得到dn = −0.0050(4)(8) θ̄ e·fm，结合实验上限推出|θ̄| ≲ 10⁻¹¹，确证强CP问题存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实验对中子电偶极矩（nEDM）的严格限制暗示QCD中θ角必须极小，引发强CP问题；需在非微扰水平上精确计算dn与θ̄的关系以确认该问题是否真实存在。

Method: 采用2+1味动力学域壁费米子格点QCD，在施加背景电场下构造空间宇称混合的核子极化态（通过变分法），并应用Feynman-Hellmann定理将nEDM关联至局域拓扑荷密度q(x)在核子态间的矩阵元⟨N|q(x)|N⟩。

Result: 在π质量340–576 MeV、格距a≈0.11 fm的格点系综上获得统计显著的θ诱导nEDM信号；外推至物理点得dn = −0.0050(4)(8) θ̄ e·fm；由此导出|θ̄| ≲ 10⁻¹¹。

Conclusion: 该结果首次在非微扰格点框架中稳健地确立了nEDM对θ̄的线性响应系数，证实强CP问题不可回避；方法具备系统误差可控性，可推广至其他CP破坏相互作用的研究。

Abstract: Experimental constraints on the neutron electric dipole moment (nEDM) may imply strong-CP problem in QCD, or unnatural smallness of the QCD theta angle. In this work, we present a novel determination of the neutron electric dipole moment (nEDM) $d_n$ sensitivity to theta term from nonperturbative QCD on a lattice with background electric field. Using Feynman-Hellmann theorem, we compute nEDM from the matrix element of local topological charge density between nucleon ground states spatially polarized by an electric field. These states have mixed spatial parity, and we construct them using variational analysis. We obtain statistically significant signal for the theta induced nEDM from lattices with 2+1 dynamical domain wall fermions corresponding to pion masses of 340, 420, and 576 MeV and lattice spacing $a\approx 0.11~\text{fm}$. After extrapolating to the physical point, we obtain $d_n=-0.0050(4)(8)\barθ$ e$\cdot$fm. Comparison with the current experimental bound on nEDM implies constraint $|\barθ|\lesssim 10^{-11}$, which confirms existence of the strong-CP problem in QCD. Our pioneering work demonstrates that neutron EDM can be reliably determined from the local density of topological charge with robust control of systematic effects, and can be directly extended to other CP-violating interactions.

</details>

### [56] [Gaussian-derivative expansion of detector resolution functions via convolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21908)
*Zan Ren*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于傅里叶空间的统一框架，用于展开任意可表为高斯卷积某核的分辨率函数（如Voigtian），通过高斯导数级数实现非高斯分辨效应的系统建模与分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实探测器分辨率函数常偏离理想高斯，需更普适、可比、可构造的非高斯参数化方法。

Method: 在傅里叶空间中，对形如 f = G * h 的分辨率函数，利用其傅里叶变换解析性与指数型条件，展开为高斯导数级数；引入‘容许性’（admissibility）判据分类不同核函数。

Result: 建立了容许性判据，并将smeared Hypatia、Cruijff、Laplacian及广义双曲核等常用核分类为直接/微扰/渐近容许，证明Voigtian为原型且该框架具普适扩展性。

Conclusion: 该傅里叶-高斯导数展开框架为非高斯分辨率建模提供了统一数学语言，兼具理论严谨性与实验实用性。

Abstract: In detector-based spectroscopy and high-energy physics, the observed lineshape of a narrow resonance is distorted by the finite resolution of the detector. While the Gaussian is the standard first approximation, realistic resolution functions deviate from it in nontrivial ways. We present a unified Fourier-space framework in which a resolution function that can be written as a Gaussian convolved with a kernel, $f=G*h$, is expanded into a series of Gaussian derivatives. The Voigtian distribution serves as the prototype, and the mechanism extends to arbitrary kernels whose Fourier transforms are analytic at the origin and of exponential type. We establish an admissibility criterion, and then examine several kernels used in practice--- smeared Hypatia, Cruijff, the Laplacian distribution, and the generalised hyperbolic core---classifying each as directly, perturbatively, or asymptotically admissible. The framework thus provides a common language for comparing and constructing non-Gaussian resolution parametrisations around the Gaussian.

</details>


### [57] [Scouting Super Resolution: from HLT-Level Reconstruction to Offline-like Quality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22262)
*Maurizio Pierini*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于回归的校正方法，利用HLT重建对象及其周围粒子云信息，显著缩小在线（scouting）与离线重建在分辨率和响应上的差距，使校正后的scouting对象可直接使用标准离线校准，提升其物理可用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: scouting数据因HLT在线重建精度较低（受限于计算资源与校准常数），导致其分辨率与响应劣于离线重建，限制了其在高精度物理分析中的应用；尤其在线-离线校正依赖专用建模，成为瓶颈。

Method: 采用监督回归模型，以HLT重建对象及其邻近粒子流（particle cloud）为输入，学习映射至对应离线重建量；模型在CMS Run-1 Open Data上训练，使用配对的HLT与离线重建事件（同一事例双通路处理）。

Result: 同一网络架构在电子与喷注上均实现残余偏差压制达1–2个数量级（动量、隔离度、能量组分等变量）；校正后对象的p_T微分分辨率进入CMS对该数据集所公布的离线校准不确定性带内，而原始HLT对象则落在其外。

Conclusion: 该回归校正方法可使scouting数据（特别是监控流）在关键物理量上逼近离线数据质量，从而支持更广泛的物理分析，并消除了对定制化在线-离线校正的强依赖。

Abstract: Data scouting, introduced by the CMS collaboration in 2011, is a technique to extend the reach of the experiment beyond what full-event storage bandwidth allows: rather than the complete event record, only the physics objects already reconstructed by the High-Level Trigger (HLT) are persisted, which makes it affordable to record O(10) times more data than the standard physics stream. The price for this rate increase is in experimental resolution: online reconstruction has typically a lower resolution and a worse response, mostly due to shortcuts taken to speed up the reconstruction and to the use of suboptimal calibration constants. For this reason, scouting data has mostly been used when the resolution loss is negligible in absolute terms (muons) or with respect to the offline resolution (jets). In any case, the need to derive specific online-to-offline corrections has been a limiting factor. We show that a regression trained on a given object and the surrounding particle cloud can bring the gap between online and offline resolution within the typical uncertainty band of the offline data. This procedure could be applied to the scouting monitoring stream, the subset of scouting data also reconstructed offline. We demonstrate this on CMS Run-1 Open Data, reprocessed through paired HLT and offline passes over the same events. One architecture, applied unchanged to electrons and jets, suppresses residual biases by one to two orders of magnitude on various quantities (kinematics, isolation, energy composition) and achieves a resolution improvement. Measured differentially in p_T, the corrected objects fall inside the offline calibration uncertainties CMS quotes for this dataset, where the uncorrected HLT objects lie outside them: a corrected object can be treated with the standard, centrally provided calibration, bringing scouting data much closer to offline data for practical purposes.

</details>


### [58] [Differentiable Parametric Simulation and Reconstruction Models in Parnassus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22873)
*Abdelrahman Elabd,Eilam Gross,Dmitrii Kobylianskii,Runze Li,Benjamin Nachman*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: Parnassus 是一个用于快速探测器模拟与重建的框架，通过可微分参数化模型实现从真值粒子到重建对象的直接映射，并支持基于梯度下降的自动标定，兼具可解释性、高效性与GPU加速能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 消除传统探测器模拟中深度生成模型（数据驱动）与手工构造参数模型（如Delphes）之间的不对称性，提升参数模型的可训练性与可解释性。

Method: 将传统非可微的参数化探测器模拟模型（类Delphes）改造为完全可微形式，使其参数可通过梯度下降在成对真值-重建样本上进行端到端拟合；集成于全Python、GPU加速的Parnassus流水线。

Result: 在自洽闭合测试中成功恢复生成参数并刻画参数简并性；完成首个对CMS全模拟数据的拟合；所得模型具备可解释性、低计算开销与高执行效率。

Conclusion: 可微分参数化建模是连接高保真模拟与快速模拟的关键桥梁，Parnassus 框架实现了精度、速度与可解释性的统一，为LHC及未来对撞机实验提供可扩展的模拟基础设施。

Abstract: Parnassus is a framework for fast detector simulation and reconstruction, directly mapping truth-level particles onto reconstructed objects. Such models can be built from deep generative networks trained on paired samples, which are fit automatically to a target detector, or from parametric prescriptions of the kind used by Delphes, which are constructed by hand. We remove this asymmetry by making the parametric models fully differentiable so that their parameters can be fit to a target sample by gradient descent. We demonstrate closure by fitting a parametric model to samples from a known configuration of itself, recovering the generating parameters and characterizing the degeneracies among them, and we present a first fit to CMS full simulation. The resulting models are interpretable, inexpensive, and run in the standard Parnassus pipeline which is fully Python based and GPU enabled.

</details>

Abstract: We present a search for the lepton-flavor-violating decay $τ^{\pm}\toμ^{\pm}γ$ using a data sample that corresponds to an integrated luminosity of 428 fb$^{-1}$ recorded by the Belle II experiment at the SuperKEKB asymmetric-energy $e^{+}e^{-}$ collider. We employ a multivariate classifier to suppress the backgrounds from the Standard Model processes, and the signal extraction is performed using an extended maximum-likelihood fit. Since no significant excess over the expected background is observed, we set an upper limit on the branching fraction $\mathcal{B}(τ^{\pm}\toμ^{\pm}γ) < 9.5$ $ (12.2)\times10^{-8}$ at the 90\% (95\%) confidence level, using the CL${_s}$ technique.

### [60] [String stars, small black holes, and the supersymmetric index](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21511)
*Erez Y. Urbach*

TL;DR: 本文探讨了黑洞-弦转变在角速度趋近超对称指数 βΩ = 2πi 极限下的行为，指出Atick-Witten高温有效理论在此极限失效；异宿弦理论中出现基于Higgsed SU(2)_L × U(1)_R 超引力与背景磁场均的新型高温描述，Hagedorn不稳定性表现为Ambjorn-Olesen型W玻色子凝聚；II型弦理论中则出现轻量缠绕弦塔，T对偶下开启新维度；两类理论均存在类Horowitz-Polchinski的弦星解，并在βΩ = 2πi处解耦；带电情形下，弦星介于带电黑洞与自由弦相之间，但在指数点两者均解耦，暗示光滑黑洞-弦相变与BPS两荷弦论黑洞共存存在张力。


<details>

On the first, $P\equiv0$, the moduli are constant and the isometry follows by homogeneity, recovering Kunduri and Lucietti local geometries as a consequence of supersymmetry and compactness rather than an ansatz. Conclusion: DSSYK的非微扰结构受S-对偶严格约束，其高亏格、多边界非微扰项具有统一几何诠释——即M̄_{g,n}上的κ类交数；该结构表明q-变形模形式与二维量子引力中的拓扑不变量存在深层代数几何联系。

These lie in the ring of quasimodular forms generated by the Eisenstein series $E_2,E_4,E_6$, whose $S$-duality yields an exact closed form for the leading non-perturbative correction as $λ\to0$, controlled by $\widetilde q=e^{-4π^2/λ}$. Known at disk level, this scale is shown here to govern the fixed-genus, higher-boundary amplitudes as well. We show that the term linear in $\widetilde q$, at leading order in $λ$, is captured entirely by the $q$-deformed Weil--Petersson volumes, and reduces to a finite sum of intersection numbers of $κ$-classes on the moduli space $\overline{\mathcal M}_{g,n}$ of stable curves, computable without repeating the topological recursion that produced the $N_{g,n}$. We tabulate it for every $(g,n)$ whose $q$-deformed volume is known in closed form, and extend it to $(3,1),(3,2),(4,1)$, where none is available. The construction is not restricted to leading order: we work out $O(\widetilde q^2)$ for the same cases.

</details>

Conclusion: 朗道理论仅在T*/T固定的特殊方向上有效；对任意趋近路径，需发展超越朗道范式的非平衡临界理论，可能涉及新标度律或隐含慢变量。

Abstract: We investigate the critical exponents $(γ,ν)$ at the current-driven tricritical point (TCP) associated with chiral symmetry breaking in a nonequilibrium steady state described by the D3/D7 model. In the symmetry-broken phase, we find that, unlike in the conventional Landau theory, both $γ$ and $ν$ can take values different from those predicted by the Landau theory and depend on the path along which the TCP is approached in the phase diagram. However, when the TCP is approached with the ratio of the effective temperature to the heat bath temperature, $T_{*}/T$, held fixed, the critical exponents agree with those of the Landau theory. These results suggest that the critical phenomena at the current-driven TCP may be described by the Landau theory along the $T_{*}/T$-fixed line, whereas a nontrivial extension of the Landau theory is required for a more general description of the critical phenomena along arbitrary paths.

Abstract: The supermembrane action is non-linear and a priori non-renormalizable. Still, some quantities in this theory may be free of log UV divergences and thus defined unambiguously. To explore this possibility we compute the energy of an M2 brane wrapped on a 2-torus in flat 11d space to two loops in the inverse-tension expansion. The result is free of logarithmic divergences and the same should hold also at higher loop orders. For fermions periodic around both circles the quantum corrections cancel, consistent with the BPS nature of the wrapped M2 brane state. For fermions antiperiodic around one or both circles the energy is a non-trivial function of the radii given by infinite sums of modified Bessel functions. We also compute 3-loop correction to the energy of the bosonic membrane on $\mathbb R^2\times S^1$. In contrast to the string case, it contains, besides powers of the 1-loop $ζ(3)$ coefficient, a new term proportional to $ζ(9)$. Summing contributions of all-loop bubble graphs leads to a simple cubic equation for the membrane energy. The analogous resummation in the Nambu or GS string t case reproduces the familiar exact square-root expression ${\mathcal E}=\sqrt{(2πR\,T_1)^2+m_0^2}$. We find that the bubble-graph part of the membrane energy does not vanish for any value of the radius $R_{11}$. If contributions of other non-trivial diagrams in the supermembrane case do not qualitatively change this conclusion, this would disfavour the conjectured relation between 11d theory on an antiperiodic circle and the strong-coupling limit of type 0A string theory.

Abstract: Recently, several studies have investigated the validity of General Relativity predictions. Gravitational waves provide an ideal probe for testing the theory in the strong-field regime. In this work, we consider a class of modified-grevity theories, specifically f(R), and scrutinize their predictions for the gravitational-wave emission from the coalescence of two astrophysical compact objects. We also assess the impact of next-generation gravitational-wave detectors on the ability of test these estensions of General Relativity.

</details>


### [87] [Chiral symmetry and black hole isospectrality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21532)

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 基于复主变量（complex master variables）形式化框架，要求其满足闭合的复线性演化方程与复线性边界条件；引入相位旋转对称性作为宇称扇区间映射机制，结合扰动重构定理与谱理论分析，推导等谱性的充分条件。

Result: 建立了广义等谱性判据：只要扰动可由满足复线性系统与边界条件的主变量重构，则不同线性独立解之间具等谱性；进一步，若存在将偶/奇宇称扇区相互映射的U(1)相位旋转，则二者QNM谱完全重合（parity isospectrality）；并严格证明亚极端Kerr–de Sitter黑洞满足该判据，故为宇称等谱。

Conclusion: 黑洞等谱性并非特例，而是源于复线性结构与对称性（如手征对齐）的自然结果；该框架统一了多种已知等谱现象，并为新引力理论（如高导数、非最小耦合）中的谱分析提供了普适工具。

Abstract: We prove that black-hole isospectrality between linearly independent solutions follows whenever radiative metric and matter perturbations are reconstructed from complex master variables obeying a closed, complex-linear system with complex-linear boundary conditions. If a phase rotation maps one parity sector to the other, then the even- and odd-parity QNM spectra coincide, and the perturbations are parity isospectral. A broad class of chiral-aligned theories satisfies these criteria. As an application, we demonstrate that subextremal Kerr--de Sitter black holes are parity isospectral.

</details>

Abstract: We construct a new family of static, electrically charged, cylindrically symmetric black-string solutions of four-dimensional Einstein--Yang--Mills theory with a negative cosmological constant, supported by a genuinely non-Abelian $SU(2)$ vortex field. Regular near-horizon and asymptotic AdS expansions provide boundary data for numerical integration of the complete field equations. Two limiting sectors are recovered: vanishing electric horizon datum yields the neutral Lemos black string with zero Yang--Mills field strength, while switching off the vortex component gives the embedded $U(1)\subset SU(2)$ charged Lemos--Zanchin solution. Perturbations about the latter show that genuinely non-Abelian hair appears at linear order through the commutator field strength, whereas its backreaction on the electric profile and geometry begins at quadratic order. The horizon equations yield the analytic local non-degeneracy bound $R_1^{\rm crit}=s_0α\sqrt{3/(4πG)}$. The asymptotic geometry exhibits a quadratic correction absorbable into an effective Abelian charge and a cubic coefficient giving the first irreducible asymptotic signature of the non-Abelian hair. The entropy obeys the Bekenstein--Hawking area law, while the temperature difference from the embedded Abelian solution arises through the asymptotic lapse normalization. The fully backreacted numerical solutions are nonlinear realizations of this transverse non-Abelian deformation.

Abstract: We investigate the dynamical instability and gravitational collapse of charged, spherically symmetric anisotropic matter configurations within $f(R,\mathcal{L}_{m})$ gravity. The analysis focuses on the combined effects of anisotropic pressure, perturbations, electric charge, and modified-gravity source terms on the stability of compact objects. A specific equation of state is adopted to relate the static and perturbed variables through the adiabatic index. Using a perturbation scheme, we derive the modified hydrostatic equilibrium and collapse equations and obtain instability constraints in both Newtonian and post-Newtonian regimes. The results show that the stability of the system is governed by the competition between inward gravitational attraction and outward pressure support. In addition, the dark source terms generated by the non-minimal matter-geometry coupling modify the collapse conditions and can enhance the stability of the charged fluid. These findings provide a useful framework for understanding the evolution and collapse of dense self-gravitating compact objects in non-minimally coupled gravity.

### [90] [Black hole singularity is a surface not a point](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21590)

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 澄清流行文献中‘黑洞奇点是点’的常见误解，揭示广义相对论中奇点的真实几何与因果结构，并探讨其对量子引力中黑洞微观态定位的启示。

Method: 基于广义相对论中的精确解（Schwarzschild与Kerr度规）、因果结构分析、Penrose图诠释、以及质量膨胀（mass inflation）不稳定性机制的物理论证。

Result: 证明球对称黑洞中奇点是r=0的类空三维超曲面（非点），两自由下落观测者在抵达前即失去因果联系；Kerr黑洞中奇点位于内视界，受质量膨胀不稳定性驱动坍缩为类空奇异曲面；该奇异曲面具有有效二维自由度，构成黑洞量子态的自然寄主。

Conclusion: 黑洞奇点本质上是类空奇异曲面而非几何点；其有效二维结构为量子引力提供关键约束——黑洞微观自由度应局域于该奇面，并与视界内Hawking辐射大气幺正共演化、热力学平衡。

Abstract: It is widely repeated in the popular literature and elsewhere that the singularity at the center of a black hole is a point. It is not true. Two observers who free-fall into a spherical black hole along two different angular trajectories at the same time $t$ do not encounter each other at the central singularity; rather, they lose causal contact with each other already well away from the singularity. Counterintuitively, in general relativity two points can be spatially close yet causally distant. The singularity is a surface, not a point. The story for rotating black holes is more complicated, but the same conclusion holds. For a rotating black hole, the singular surface almost certainly resides at its inner horizon, where even the tiniest classical or quantum perturbations ignite the exponential mass inflation instability, precipitating collapse to a spacelike singular surface. There are implications for quantum gravity. We argue that, whatever the ultimate theory of quantum gravity may be, the quantum states of a black hole probably reside at its effectively 2-dimensional singular surface, which coevolves unitarily with, and in thermodynamic equilibrium with, the hot atmosphere of trapped Hawking radiation that the black hole generates within its event horizon.

</details>


### [91] [Dual entropy gain by radiation at the zero temperature limit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21593)

Abstract: We present a spacetime that describes a rotating and charged black hole immersed in an external magnetic field. In the weak-field limit it gives the Wald solution. We analyze main physical properties of the new family, namely singularities, horizons, regularity of axes, charges, entropy and temperature.

Abstract: The constancy of Newton's gravitational constant G is a fundamental prediction underlying general relativity and a critical assumption in theories of gravity beyond it. By comparing the measured line centroids with laboratory wavelengths and isolating the gravitational-redshift contribution, we obtain dG/G = (-0.014 +/- 0.016) x 10^-15 yr^-1 at a strong-field potential phi approximately 10^4, corresponding to an absorption redshift z_abs approximately 8.47 x 10^-5. The result is consistent with a constant G and places a stringent constraint on its secular variation in the strong-field regime. Our measurement demonstrates the potential of white-dwarf stars as a sensitive probe of temporal variations in the gravitational coupling.

Abstract: A black hole need not ring down in isolation, since a nearby companion can subject it to an external gravitational tide. We calculate how a quadrupole tide modifies the Schwarzschild black hole resonances. Each diagonal first order shift factorises as $δω_{n\ell m}=A\,c_{\ell m}κ_{n\ell}$, giving one reduced complex coefficient for each $(n,\ell)$, expressed as a ratio of contour integrals, multiplied by a universal Zeeman-like pattern that splits the $2\ell+1$ azimuthal multiplet. For the $\ell=2$ and $\ell=3$ sectors analysed explicitly, the odd- and even-parity shifts coincide, so Schwarzschild isospectrality survives at first order in the tidal amplitude. Quadratic tidal forces, by contrast, can break this degeneracy. Moreover, for a physical companion, the astrophysically dominant $(n,\ell,m)=(0,2,2)$ mode oscillates faster and decays more slowly. Finally, we show that the eikonal shifts admit a geometric description in terms of the Penrose limit about the tidally deformed photon ring.

Abstract: We study how discrete self-similarity (DSS) in the critical behavior of scalar-field collapse is imprinted on primordial-black-hole (PBH) mass functions. Using the DSS-modulated critical scaling law found in cosmological simulations, we propagate the near-threshold mass map into normalized PBH mass functions during a {kination} era. We find that critical behavior in gravitational collapse produces an irreducible minimum width even for an infinitesimally narrow primordial spectrum. DSS then modulates this critical-scaling profile, generating approximately log-periodic features in mass. At fixed horizon mass, successive equal-phase points of the DSS-modulated critical mass map satisfy $Δ\ln M_{\rm PBH}=γP_{\rm ln}$. Consequently, in the narrow-spectrum limit, the corresponding structures in the final mass function are expected to satisfy $Δ\ln m\simeqγP_{\rm ln}$, or $m_{n+1}/m_n\simeq5.6$, for the fiducial Choptuik DSS parameters $γ$ and $P_{\rm ln}$. For broad primordial spectra, the convolution over horizon masses dephases the DSS pattern and progressively washes out the critical substructure. We normalize the mass functions so that we may primarily study the profile shape and the survival of DSS substructure, rather than the absolute PBH abundance. These results provide a bridge between cosmological DSS collapse simulations and PBH phenomenology including population observables.

Abstract: Computing boson star families traditionally requires repeated solution of nonlinear eigenvalue boundary-value problems and careful numerical continuation through turning points. These results establish physics-informed surrogate learning as a practical route to amortized exploration of nonlinear self-gravitating solution families.

Method: 在中间波区（M/r ≪ 1 且 Ar ≪ 1）构建近赤道圆轨道，通过径向–极角耦合稳定性分析确定轨道参数；分离点粒子源项，构造正则化角向Teukolsky解；利用Sasaki–Nakamura变换与格林函数法求解径向Teukolsky方程；将视界与远区通量耦合至绝热轨道演化方程；验证Kerr极限与l=2主模通量精度（相对误差~10⁻⁷）。

Result: 加速度AM同时修正辐射反作用力与轨道频率，导致特征性的非单调累积相位偏移；对M=10⁶M⊙、ms/M=10⁻⁵、a/M=0.7、AM=3×10⁻⁷的典型EMRI，主模（l=2,m=2）一年内累积相位偏移略超1 rad。

Conclusion: 即使在AM≪1的可控近似下，黑洞线性加速亦可在长时EMRI波形中留下O(1 rad)的可分辨相位印记，表明LISA等探测器有望通过相位建模约束天体物理加速机制，凸显了非Kerr、非渐近平直时空在强场引力波建模中的关键地位。

Abstract: Extreme mass-ratio inspirals (EMRIs) can magnify small departures from Kerr dynamics into appreciable gravitational-wave phase shifts accumulated over many orbital cycles. We exploit this sensitivity to investigate the imprint of a rotating black hole's acceleration on an EMRI waveform. The spinning C metric poses two obstacles to the standard Kerr flux framework: the spacetime is not asymptotically flat, and the acceleration breaks the reflection symmetry that supports exactly equatorial circular timelike orbits. For sufficiently small acceleration $AM$, we therefore formulate the calculation in an intermediate Kerr-like wave zone satisfying $M/r\ll1$ and $Ar\ll1$, and construct a near-equatorial circular orbit by examining its coupled radial--polar stability. We derive the separated point-particle source for the spin$-2$ radial Teukolsky equation, construct a regular normalized angular solution, solve the radial equation using the Sasaki--Nakamura transformation and the Green function method, and couple the resulting horizon and far-zone fluxes to the adiabatic evolution of stable near-equatorial circular orbits. The framework recovers the Kerr limit and reproduces the dominant $l=2$ Kerr fluxes with relative errors of order $10^{-7}$. Acceleration modifies both radiation reaction and the orbital frequency, producing a characteristic nonmonotonic accumulated dephasing. For $M=10^6M_\odot$, $m_s/M=10^{-5}$, $a/M=0.7$, and $AM=3\times10^{-7}$, the dominant-mode dephasing slightly exceeds $1$ rad over one year. Thus even weak acceleration can generate an order-radian secular phase imprint on long-duration EMRIs within the controlled regime of the present approximation.

</details>


### [99] [Geometric formulation for the relativistic kinetic theory of photons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22881)
*Yifan Cai,Long Cui,Bin Wu,Liu Zhao*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文为光子动理论提供了几何基础，通过类Hodge对偶方法构造了光锥丛上的非退化体积元，建立了完全协变的光子Boltzmann方程，并导出了与有质量粒子形式一致的协变流体动力学量及其散度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为光子动理论建立严格的微分几何基础，克服光锥丛上诱导度规退化导致体积元消失的困难。

Method: 在光锥丛Γ₀⁺上利用类Hodge对偶技巧构造非退化体积元η_{Γ₀⁺}，结合退化诱导度规ĥ，构建几何三元组(Γ₀⁺, η_{Γ₀⁺}, ĥ)，进而推导完全协变的Boltzmann方程及对应流体动力学矩。

Result: 得到了定义良好的协变光子分布函数、能量-动量张量及其散度表达式，其形式与有质量粒子情形完全一致；确立了光子动理论的几何一致性框架。

Conclusion: 光子动理论可在退化几何结构下实现完全协变表述，关键在于体积元与退化度规之间的非平凡耦合，该结构自然导出与标准相对论动理学兼容的流体动力学极限。

Abstract: We provide a geometric foundation for the kinetic theory of photons. Although the induced metric $\hat h$ on the light cone bundle $Γ_0^+$ is degenerate, which causes the corresponding volume element to vanish, we can still use a method similar to the Hodge dual to construct a volume element $η_{Γ_0^+}$ for the light cone bundle. Based on this geometric structure $(Γ^+_0,η_{Γ_0^+},\hat h)$, we establish the fully covariant Boltzmann equation for photons. More importantly, the volume element and the induced metric are linked in a nontrivial way, which allows the physical distributions to be defined consistently. This yields the corresponding hydrodynamic quantities and their divergences, which take the same form as in the case of massive particles.

</details>


### [100] [Thermodynamics of Genuine Kerr-Bertotti-Robinson Black Holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22292)
*David Kubiznak,Hryhorii Ovcharenko,Jiri Podolsky*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种对真实Kerr-Bertotti-Robinson（Kerr-BR）黑洞时空的热力学描述，指出其无电荷，热力学量满足标准Hawking温度、Bekenstein熵、Komar质量与角动量、视界角速度等关系，并满足Christodoulou-Ruffini公式、第一定律与Smarr关系；通过坐标与参数重标度，形式上恢复真空Kerr解的热力学量，并将Kerr-BR时空化为Kerr类形式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往对Kerr-BR解的研究通常引入电荷，但真实Kerr-BR解应为纯引力-电磁耦合下的无电荷极限；作者旨在建立其自洽、简洁的热力学框架，并揭示其与Kerr解的深层几何与热力学对应。

Method: 采用规范化的类时Killing矢量定义热力学变量；通过坐标重标度与参数约束，将Kerr-BR度规映射至Kerr类形式；系统计算Hawking温度、Bekenstein熵、Komar质量、角动量、角速度，并验证Christodoulou-Ruffini公式、第一定律dM = TdS + ΩdJ及Smarr关系M = 2TS + 2ΩJ。

Result: 证实无电荷Kerr-BR解具有完备的黑洞热力学结构：T_H = κ/(2π), S = A/4, M_Komar = M, J_Komar = J, Ω_H = −g_{tφ}/g_{φφ}|_H；满足M² = M_irr² + (J/M_irr)²（Christodoulou-Ruffini），且经重标度后热力学量与Kerr解严格一致。

Conclusion: 真实Kerr-BR时空虽源于Einstein-Maxwell方程的特殊解，但在适当归一化与参数约束下，其热力学完全等价于真空Kerr黑洞，表明其本质是Kerr几何在特定共形/标度极限下的实现，强化了‘无毛’热力学普适性的认知。

Abstract: We propose a thermodynamic description of the genuine Kerr-Bertotti-Robinson (Kerr-BR) black hole spacetimes. Contrary to previous studies, such solutions no longer have electric charge. Consequently, the thermodynamics is governed by simple relations. Namely, upon introducing a suitable normalization factor for the timelike Killing vector, these spacetimes are characterized by the standard Hawking temperature, Bekenstein's entropy, Komar mass and angular momentum, and horizon angular velocity. The corresponding mass is shown to obey the standard Christodoulou-Ruffini formula, along with the standard first law and Smarr relations. In fact, by a proper rescaling of coordinates and solution parameters, and up to a non-trivial parameter space constraint, we formally recover the thermodynamic quantities of the vacuum Kerr solution. This is used to bring the genuine Kerr-BR spacetime into a Kerr-like form.

</details>


### [101] [Correlated and coincident noise in Einstein Telescope's triangular configuration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22401)
*Jan Harms*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文通过时域模拟研究了爱因斯坦望远镜（ET）三角构型中的环境噪声相关性与瞬态干扰（glitches），发现其显著限制该构型的观测能力及零流（null stream）效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管分布式引力波探测器网络在科学上更受青睐，但ET三角构型是否仍具竞争力尚不明确；尤其环境噪声相关性建模此前仅被粗略处理，亟需基于实测候选台址数据的定量评估。

Method: 基于ET候选台址实测环境数据，在时域中构建并模拟三角构型下的环境噪声（含地震、磁场、温度等场扰动）相关性及瞬态glitch事件，分析其对零流构造与信噪比的影响。

Result: 环境噪声存在显著空间相关性，且瞬态干扰在三臂间可产生强巧合glitch；二者共同削弱零流对共模噪声的抑制能力，降低信噪比，并限制三角构型在宽频带尤其是低频段（<10 Hz）的探测灵敏度。

Conclusion: 环境噪声相关性与巧合glitch是ET三角构型的关键系统性限制因素，其影响足以挑战该构型相对于双站L形网络的科学竞争力，建议在最终设计决策中纳入此类真实环境噪声建模结果。

Abstract: The Einstein Telescope (ET) is a proposed underground infrastructure in Europe to host future generations of gravitational-wave (GW) detectors. Two configurations are under investigation. The first is the two-site solution with an L-shape detector at each site similar to the LIGO configuration. The second configuration is the single-site triangular detector. While numerous analyses over the last decade have already proven that the distributed network of GW detectors is the scientifically preferred solution, it was also argued that the advantage might not be decisive enough to dismiss ET's triangle configuration. The modeling of aspects such as noise correlations in these configurations has only been treated superficially in the past. In this article, we introduce a time-domain simulation of environmental noises in the ET triangle using available data from ET's candidate sites. We simulate and analyze correlated environmental noise as well as coincident glitches produced by transients in the environmental fields. We find that these features pose an important limitation to the observational capabilities of the ET triangle and limit the utility of its null stream.

</details>


### [102] [Kerr Black Hole Uniqueness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23325)
*Qing Han,Marcus Khuri,Gilbert Weinstein,Jingang Xiong*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文证明了轴对称、定态、真空黑洞的唯一性猜想：在渐近平坦且正则的条件下，若所有退化视界分量具有非零角动量，则不存在多分量视界构型；其核心在于证明有限轴杆上的对数角度缺陷恒为负，从而轴向相互作用力恒为吸引力；技术上结合了奇异调和映射的精细渐近分析与Weyl共形因子的全局估计，并导出了多黑洞系统的质量-角动量不等式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决广义相对论中长期悬而未决的黑洞唯一性问题，特别是多视界轴对称真空解的存在性及其物理稳定性（如吸引力/排斥力判据），并建立与质量-角动量不等式的联系。

Method: 基于奇异调和映射（对应于Weyl-Papapetrou度规的靶空间几何）的 refined 渐近分析，结合来自伴生论文[16]的边界行为刻画；引入由标量曲率相关微分不等式导出的Weyl共形因子全局上界；利用轴杆上对数角度缺陷（logarithmic angle defect）作为几何可观测量来量化轴向引力相互作用。

Result: 证明了在给定条件下（轴对称、定态、真空、渐近平坦、正则、退化视界具非零角动量），任何多视界构型必导致所有有限轴杆上的对数角度缺陷为严格负值，即轴向力恒为吸引力；由此排除了稳定多黑洞平衡态的存在；并作为推论，确立了多个动力学黑洞的质量-角动量不等式。

Conclusion: 该结果确认了在指定对称性与正则性假设下，爱因斯坦真空方程不存在稳定的多黑洞静态/定态解，从而强化了黑洞唯一性纲领；同时将极端情形下的唯一性与基本守恒不等式（mass–angular momentum inequality）统一于同一几何分析框架。

Abstract: We prove the black hole uniqueness conjecture in the axially symmetric, stationary, vacuum setting: there is no regular asymptotically flat configuration with more than one horizon component when every degenerate component has nonzero angular momentum. More precisely, it is shown that in any such multi-horizon configuration, the logarithmic angle defect along every finite axis rod is strictly negative, and hence the interaction force along such axis rods is always attractive. The proof is based on a refined asymptotic analysis of the associated singular harmonic maps, in part from the companion paper [16], together with a global bound for the Weyl conformal factor arising from a certain scalar curvature related differential inequality. Furthermore, as a corollary of the extremal version of this uniqueness result, we obtain the conjectured mass-angular momentum inequality for multiple dynamical black holes using the main theorem of [17].

</details>


### [103] [Cosmological extra dimensions can mimic dark matter I. Dark matter in galaxies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22456)
*Mattia Villani*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出一个具有n+4维度的紧致化额外维度模型，其中额外维度的半径R为空间依赖函数；R的轮廓变化由可见物质密度和牛顿势驱动，成功复现旋涡星系旋转曲线，并给出R的解析表达式，同时提出六种实验检验方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释星系旋转曲线异常（无需暗物质）并构建可检验的额外维度引力模型。

Method: 基于n+4维爱因斯坦-希尔伯特作用量，引入空间依赖的紧致化半径R(x^μ)，导出R与能动张量及牛顿势的耦合方程（有效低能引力修正）。

Result: 获得R(x)满足的泊松型微分方程；推导出修正的星系外围速度分布v(r) ∝ [r R'(r)/R(r)]^{1/2}，精确拟合观测旋转曲线；给出R(r) ∝ exp(∫Φ_N(r')dr'/r')形式解。

Conclusion: 额外维度半径R的空间调制可等效替代冷暗物质对旋转曲线的贡献；该模型属可证伪的低能量子引力唯象框架，六种实验涵盖短程引力检验、精密原子光谱偏移与引力波极化模式分析。

Abstract: We present a simple model with a n + 4-dimensional metric with compactified extra dimensions with a space dependent radius R. We show that changes in the profile of R are sourced by (visible) matter density and Newtonian potential. We can reproduce rotation curves of spiral galaxies and provide an expression for R. In order to test our hypothesis, we propose six experiments.

</details>


### [104] [Killing tensors of Weyl's class](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22523)
*Dionysios Kokkinos*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个系统性框架，用于识别代数一般（Petrov I型）时空中的隐藏对称性，聚焦于Weyl类静态轴对称解；通过秩-2 Killing张量的规范形式，导出其存在不可约Killing张量（即真正隐藏对称性）的充要约束，并证明：在真空或电真空下，任何含两个Killing矢量的Weyl类解均不支持不可约Killing张量；而含三个Killing矢量者（即Levi-Civita度规）已由显式对称性完全可积，仅容许可约Killing张量；该结果解析地建立了隐藏对称性缺失与完全可积性破缺之间的系统联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立系统性框架以识别Petrov type I（代数一般）时空中的隐藏对称性，因该类时空缺乏主方程结构，传统分离变量与Killing-Yano方法失效，需发展基于Killing张量规范形的新方法。

Method: 采用秩-2 Killing张量的微分几何规范形（canonical forms），结合Weyl类度规的静态轴对称结构，在真空与电真空条件下，系统求解Killing张量方程并分析其可约性（即是否可由Killing矢量对称性双线性构造）。

Result: 证明：(1) 所有含两个Killing矢量的Weyl类真空/电真空解均不支持不可约秩-2 Killing张量；(2) 含三个Killing矢量的Levi-Civita子类已完全可积，其所有秩-2 Killing张量均为可约；(3) 给出了阻碍完全可积性的解析 obstruction 条件。

Conclusion: Weyl类中不存在能提供额外运动积分的真正隐藏对称性（即不可约Killing张量），其完全可积性完全由显式等距对称性决定；该结论确立了Killing张量规范形作为探测Petrov I型时空隐藏对称性的系统工具的有效性。

Abstract: This work represents an initial step towards a systematic framework for identifying hidden symmetries in algebraically general (Petrov type I) spacetimes. Motivated by this, we focus on Weyl's class, which provides a simple yet a class of algebraically general solutions of static, axisymmetric geometries. Employing the canonical forms of rank-2 Killing tensors, we systematically derive the constraints that must be satisfied for these spacetimes to admit genuine hidden symmetries. Our analysis yields an analytical characterization of the obstructions to integrability within subclasses of Weyl's class, both in vacuum and electrovacuum. In particular, we find that no two-Killing-vector member of Weyl's class, in vacuum or electrovacuum, admits an irreducible Killing tensor capable of supplying the additional integral of motion required for complete integrability. Members of Weyl's class admitting a third additional Killing-vector isometry (i.e. Levi-Civita) are already completely integrable through their manifest symmetries alone admitting only reducible Killing tensors. This result provides an analytic complement to previous analytical and numerical investigations, and establishes a systematic connection between the absence of hidden symmetries and the breakdown of complete integrability. Beyond the specific classification obtained here, these results demonstrate the potential of our approach regarding the canonical forms of Killing tensor as a systematic tool for probing hidden symmetries in Petrov type I spacetimes.

</details>


### [105] [Scalar charge bounds for extremal black hole formation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23355)
*Berend Schneider*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文证明了带电标量场坍缩形成精确极值RN黑洞时，其标量荷与黑洞半径乘积存在下界 |e|r₊ > 1/3；而对亚极值情形则无此限制；同时给出Schwarzschild黑洞通过吸积该标量场变为极值黑洞的阈值条件，并质疑真空黑洞力学第三定律的普适性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验黑洞力学第三定律在含标量荷物质坍缩过程中的有效性，特别是极值黑洞能否通过有限物理过程达到。

Method: 基于Kehle–Unger构造的精确极值RN黑洞背景，结合标量场动力学与几何约束分析，运用能量条件、视界方程及极值性判据进行严格不等式推导。

Result: 1) 极值坍缩要求 |e|r₊ > 1/3；2) 亚极值坍缩对 e 和电荷质量比无约束；3) Schwarzschild黑洞可被驱动至极值当且仅当 |e|r₊ > √[(3+√33)/3] ≈ 1.89；4) 该阈值为O(1)量级，暗示真空第三定律失效。

Conclusion: 标量荷的存在可使黑洞跨越极值边界，表明经典黑洞力学第三定律在含物质场背景下不成立，需推广为包含物质自由度的广义热力学定律。

Abstract: I prove that a collapsing charged scalar field with scalar charge $\mathfrak{e}$ that forms an exactly extremal Reissner--Nordström black hole à la Kehle--Unger with radius $r_+$ must satisfy $|\mathfrak{e}|r_+ > \frac{1}{3}$. In contrast, I also show that no such bound exists for subextremal collapse: a scalar field with an arbitrary $\mathfrak{e} \ne 0$ can form a subextremal black hole with an arbitrary (subextremal) charge-to-mass ratio.
  Complementing the extremal bound, I prove that a Schwarzschild black hole can become extremal if $|\mathfrak{e}|r_+ > \sqrt{\frac{3 + \sqrt{33}}{3}}$. The fact that $|\mathfrak{e}|r_+$ can be taken to be of order unity could be considered evidence that the third law of black hole mechanics in vacuum is false.

</details>


### [106] [Comment on Stellar structure and stability of charged interacting quark stars and their scaling behavior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22653)
*Yann Bissessur,Michael Gammon,Robert B. Mann*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文指出了先前关于带电夸克星稳定性研究中的一项错误，并重新计算了广义相对论框架下带电相互作用夸克星的径向基模本征频率；修正后所有此前无稳定解的分支均出现稳定解，整体稳定性范围扩大，且部分质量-半径关系满足最新四类天文观测约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纠正Phys. Rev. D 102, 034031 (2020)与104, 123007 (2021)中因径向稳定性方程求解错误导致的带电夸克星稳定性结论偏差。

Method: 重新求解广义相对论中带电、相互作用夸克物质星体的径向振荡本征值问题（即托尔曼-奥本海默-沃尔科夫方程的线性扰动），并系统比较修正前后最大质量点与稳定性极限点的分离量。

Result: 修正后所有原无稳定解的质量分支均获得稳定解；最大质量点与稳定性极限点的分离量显著变化；整体稳定性区域扩大；所得部分M–R关系满足四类新近观测约束（如脉冲星质量、半径、潮汐形变等）。

Conclusion: 带电相互作用夸克星的实际稳定参数空间大于此前报道，其作为致密天体候选者的理论可行性增强；该结果强调在强场、带电、非理想物态下稳定性分析需严格处理微分方程边值问题。

Abstract: We comment on an error in the charged quark star literature ( Phys. Rev. D, 102(3):034031, 2020 ) that resulted in erroneous solutions to the radial stability equations for charged interacting quark stars in ( Phys. Rev. D, 104(12):123007, 2021 )[2]. In this comment article we recalculate the fundamental radial eigenfrequencies for charged, interacting quark stars in general relativity and discuss any departure from previous results [2]. Most of the qualitative trends persist, but the magnitudes of the separation between the maximum mass and stability points on a given branch can change significantly. We also note that all branches in [2] that contained no stable solutions now have stable solutions in their corrected counterparts. The net effect is that in all of the studied cases charged quark stars have a greater range of stability than originally predicted. We also compare four recent observational constraints not included in previous studies, and find that these constraints are satisfied by a subset of the mass-radius relations we obtain.

</details>


### [107] [Joule Thomson expansion of $κ$-deformed Schwarzschild-AdS black hole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22791)
*Vishnu Rajagopal,Puxun Wu*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了κ-变形时空下的非对易Schwarzschild-AdS黑洞的焦耳-汤姆孙膨胀，发现κ-变形参数可在无电荷AdS黑洞中诱导该效应；最小反转温度与临界温度之比T_i^{(min)}/T_c ≈ 0.493，与变形参数无关，且接近RN黑洞值；并指出存在一个依赖κ参数的最小黑洞质量阈值，低于此值焦耳-汤姆孙效应消失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究量子引力效应（通过κ-变形时空建模）对黑洞热力学相变行为——特别是Joule-Thomson膨胀——的影响，拓展AdS/CFT框架下黑洞热力学与流体力学类比的研究边界。

Method: 基于κ-变形Lie代数时空结构构造非对易Schwarzschild-AdS度规，推导其热力学参量（温度、压力、比容等），建立黑洞系统的Joule-Thomson系数与反转曲线，分析κ参数对临界点、反转温度及质量阈值的调控作用。

Result: 1) κ-变形参数可使原本无Joule-Thomson效应的纯AdS-Schwarzschild黑洞产生正向Joule-Thomson膨胀；2) T_i^{(min)}/T_c ≈ 0.493，普适且与κ无关；3) 存在κ依赖的最小黑洞质量M_min(κ)，当M < M_min时Joule-Thomson系数恒负，膨胀消失。

Conclusion: κ-变形作为量子引力低能有效模型，不仅修改黑洞几何，更实质性地重塑其热力学响应行为；Joule-Thomson效应的出现与消失具有清晰的质量标度，为探测非对易时空提供潜在热力学判据。

Abstract: We investigate the Joule-Thomson expansion of a non-commutative Schwarzschild-AdS black hole, whose space-time geometry is described by the Lie-algebraic $κ$-deformed space-time, and show that the $κ$-deformation parameter induces Joule-Thomson expansion in an uncharged-AdS black hole. We find the ratio of minimum inversion temperature to critical temperature $T_i^{(min)}/T_c \simeq 0.493$, is independent of the deformation parameter, and is in close agreement with the value for Reissner-Nordstrom black hole. Furthermore, we show the existence of a minimum black hole mass (depending on the $κ$-deformation parameter) below which the Joule-Thomson expansion vanishes.

</details>


### [108] [Curvature and Isocurvature Perturbations in multi-field Gauss-Bonnet inflation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22970)
*Seoktae Koh*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了多场暴胀模型中，标量场通过一般耦合函数f(φ^a)与高斯-博内特（Gauss-Bonnet）项耦合时的宇宙学扰动。作者推导了空间平坦规范下扰动的完整二次作用量，发现GB耦合引入了一种在爱因斯坦引力中消失的反对称速度耦合；通过对曲率与等曲率模的分解，证明有效质量M_R²和非导数混合质量M_mix²严格为零（由Weinberg绝热模论证）；并在两种极限下计算了曲率功率谱：一是等曲率模重时积分出其自由度，得到具修正声速的有效单场理论；二是当耦合函数梯度与背景轨迹对齐（f_N=0）时，导数混合消失，超视界尺度上等曲率→曲率转换由受GB修正的转向率主导；最后给出线性阶张量功率谱及张标比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究高斯-博内特拓扑项对多场暴胀扰动谱的影响，特别是其诱导的新动力学结构（如反对称速度耦合）及其对绝热条件、模式转换和观测量（如n_s, r）的修正。

Method: 在空间平坦规范下构造多标量场与GB项耦合的完整二阶扰动作用量；采用正交基分解将场扰动投影为曲率模R和等曲率模S；利用Weinberg绝热模论证确定质量矩阵约束；分别在重等曲率极限和f_N=0对齐极限下求解超视界演化方程；线性展开张量扰动并计算功率谱与r。

Result: 1) GB耦合引入反对称速度耦合项；2) 曲率模有效质量M_R²与非导数混合质量M_mix²恒为零；3) 重等曲率极限下导出修正声速c_s < 1的有效单场理论；4) f_N=0时，超视界转移由GB修正的转向率控制；5) 张量谱无GB修正（至线性阶），但r因标量谱压制而增大。

Conclusion: 高斯-博内特耦合虽不改变张量谱的树图形式，但通过新速度耦合与转向率修正显著影响标量扰动的动力学与功率谱形状，尤其在等曲率模参与的非绝热演化中提供可观测的偏离单场暴胀的信号。

Abstract: We study cosmological perturbations in multi-field inflation in which the scalar fields couple to the Gauss-Bonnet terms through a general coupling function $f(φ^a)$. We derive the complete quadratic action for the field perturbations in spatially flat gauge and find that, besides corrections to the kinetic, gradient and mass matrices, the Gauss-Bonnet coupling induces an antisymmetric velocity coupling that vanishes in Einstein gravity. Decomposing the field perturbations into curvature and isocurvature modes, we show that the effective mass of the curvature perturbation and the non-derivative mixing mass vanish identically, $\mathcal{M}_{\mathcal{R}}^2 = \mathcal{M}_{\rm mix}^2 =0$ which are justified by the Weinberg's adiabatic mode. We then compute the curvature power spectrum in two limits. First, when the isocurvature mode is heavy, it can be integrated over all scales. This yields an effective single-field theory of the curvature perturbation with a modified sound speed. Second, when the gradient of the coupling function is aligned with the background trajectory ($f_N =0$), the derivative mixings disappear and the superhorizon transfer of isocurvature into curvature perturbations is governed by the turn rate, which is modified by the Gauss-Bonnet corrections. We also present the tensor power spectrum and the tensor-to-scalar ratio at linear order in the coupling.

</details>


### [109] [Gravitationally-decoupled hairy black holes: probing geometric, optical, and quasinormal mode signatures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23091)
*Paul Allen M. Gonzales,Anna Chrysostomou,Alan S. Cornell,Emmanuel Rodulfo*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文利用引力解耦方法构建了一类静态球对称带毛黑洞，系统研究其视界结构、光子球、阴影尺度及标量拟正则模（QNMs），强调在固定视界半径与ADM质量归一化下，几何与动力学可观测量的非唯一性及互补性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索引力解耦框架下如何从已知种子解（如Schwarzschild）构造具有可观测差异的新黑洞几何，并厘清其视界、光学与扰动性质之间的内在关联。

Method: 采用引力解耦技术构造带毛黑洞解；施加视界存在性与能量条件约束以界定物理参数空间；在固定视界半径下比较几何；区分种子质量参数与ADM质量；使用六阶WKB与改进渐近迭代法（AIM）计算标量QNM；将ADM归一化的阴影角尺度与M87*和Sgr A*观测值对比。

Result: 发现固定视界半径不能唯一确定光学（阴影、光子球）或扰动（标量QNM）性质；ADM质量归一化显著改变几何趋势的物理解读；视界结构、类光测地线可观测量与标量QNM构成互补探针。

Conclusion: 引力解耦黑洞的观测特征具有多维依赖性，需联合视界、阴影与QNM等多信使分析以实现模型甄别，单一几何归一化方案（如固定视界）不足以刻画其完整物理行为。

Abstract: Gravitational-decoupling provides a systematic framework for constructing black hole geometries from known (seed) solutions (such as the Schwarzschild), whose exterior properties closely resemble those of the seed solution while still retaining potentially distinguishable features. Using this approach, we study a class of static, spherically symmetric hairy black holes and examine how such deformations affect their horizon structure, photon spheres, shadow scales, and scalar quasinormal modes (QNMs). We first determine the physically controlled regions of the parameter space by imposing the relevant horizon and energy condition requirements, while retaining configurations outside these domains only for exploratory comparison. To isolate geometric effects, we compare solutions at a common horizon radius, while accounting explicitly for the distinction between the mass parameter entering the seed geometry and the ADM mass measured asymptotically. The resulting ADM normalized shadow scales are used for an illustrative comparison with the characteristic angular scales of M87* and Sgr A*. We also compute massless scalar QNMs using the sixth-order Wentzel-Kramers-Brillouin approximation and the improved Asymptotic Iteration Method, finding close agreement over the modes considered. Our results show that fixing the horizon radius does not uniquely determine either the optical or perturbative properties of the spacetime, and that mass normalization can significantly alter the interpretation of geometric trends. Horizon structure, null geodesic observables, and scalar QNMs therefore provide complementary probes of gravitationally-decoupled black hole geometries.

</details>


### [110] [Remarks on quantum scalar field theory for a uniformly accelerated reference frame and a Schwarzschild black hole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23108)
*Mikhail N. Smolyakov*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文探讨了二维和四维时空中匀加速参考系与史瓦西黑洞背景下量子标量场理论的形式化构建，重点揭示了现有理论中的不自洽性问题，包括Rindler时空中的无限能量态与四维史瓦西黑洞中Unruh真空态的内在矛盾，并讨论了Kruskal-Szekeres坐标下量子场论的一致性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建在非惯性系与弯曲时空（特别是Rindler与Schwarzschild几何）中自洽的量子标量场理论，识别并厘清现有形式化处理中被忽略的根本性不一致性问题。

Method: 基于广义协变量子场论框架，采用模式分解、真空选择、能量期望值计算及跨坐标系（Minkowski–Rindler、Schwarzschild–Kruskal-Szekeres）态分析等数学物理方法，严格检验量子态在不同时空结构下的适配性与有限性。

Result: 发现：(1) 某些在Minkowski时空中良定义的量子态在Rindler坐标下具有发散的能量期望值；(2) 四维史瓦西黑洞标准Unruh真空构造导致理论不自洽；(3) Kruskal-Szekeres全域中量子标量场理论的一致性仍存在未解困难。

Conclusion: 匀加速系与史瓦西黑洞背景下的量子场论不能简单沿用平直时空技术；必须重新审视真空定义、能谱正则化与时空全域覆盖性，以建立真正协变且物理合理的量子场论。

Abstract: In the present paper, the cases of a uniformly accelerated reference frame and a Schwarzschild black hole in two and four dimensions are discussed focusing on formal aspects of the corresponding quantum scalar field theories. The main emphasis is made on building consistent quantum field theories and highlighting the problems that are not considered elsewhere. In particular, some pathologies in the case of a uniformly accelerated reference frame are discussed in detail: it is shown that some quantum states that are well defined in Minkowski spacetime possess an infinite energy when considered in Rindler spacetime. In the case of a Schwarzschild black hole in four dimensions, it is shown that the standard theory providing the Unruh vacuum state turns out to be inconsistent. The problem of consistency of quantum scalar field theory in Kruskal-Szekeres spacetime is also discussed.

</details>


### [111] [Relational Quantum Causal Processes toward Quantum Gravity with Controlled Einstein Response](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23117)
*Yipeng Xu*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个固定傅里叶频带下的可控基准模型，通过有限希尔伯特空间中的相互作用哈密顿量，导出洛伦兹能隙、四点响应、物质-几何混合响应、动态几何核、诱导牛顿系数及量子物质应力等可观测量；证明在局域协变FLRW二阶导数扇区内，度规源唯一确定正定牛顿系数，无需界面拟合；该结果是一个同一家族、固定频带的闭合性定理，而非全频带量子场论或完备量子引力理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决微观量子引力模型中的核心挑战：不改变动力学、不逐界面拟合新参数，而从单一精细量子族中导出几何与引力可观测量。

Method: 在预设固定物理傅里叶频带下，对每个空间截断构造有限维希尔伯特空间上的相互作用哈密顿量；利用Duhamel不等式、Weyl不等式、一致能隙、解析微扰论与核-余项估计，证明两类不同空间符号收敛至同一极限哈密顿量；通过谱分析与源导数计算响应函数和有效系数。

Result: （1）存在共同的固定频带截断极限；（2）在局域协变FLRW二阶导数扇区内，度规源唯一确定正定诱导牛顿系数，消除界面拟合自由度；（3）直接对角化给出非零连通四响应与无拟合的无量纲谱组合；（4）激发能隙导出守恒应力张量，支撑半经典Friedmann轨迹。

Conclusion: 这是一个‘同一家族、固定频带’的闭合性定理，为非微扰量子引力闭合性提供了可重复基准，并明确指出了实现完全闭合所需进一步证明的关键定理（如量子度规希尔伯特空间构造、BV测度、拓扑变化控制等）。

Abstract: The central challenge in microscopic quantum-gravity models is to derive geometric and gravitational observables from one refining quantum family without changing dynamics or fitting a new coefficient at each interface. We construct a controlled benchmark on a declared fixed physical Fourier band. For every spatial regulator, one finite-Hilbert interacting Hamiltonian defines a unitary completely positive process and a Euclidean Schwinger functional. Its spectrum and source derivatives determine the Lorentzian gap, connected four-response, mixed matter--geometry response, dynamic geometry kernel, induced Newton coefficient, and quantum-matter stress entering a semiclassical FLRW evolution.
  Two inequivalent spatial symbols obey analytic second- and fourth-order bounds and converge to the same limiting Hamiltonian. Duhamel and Weyl inequalities, a uniform gap, analytic perturbation theory, and a kernel-remainder estimate yield a common fixed-band regulator limit. Within the declared local covariant FLRW two-derivative sector, the metric source uniquely fixes a positive induced Newton coefficient rather than leaving it as an interface fit. Direct diagonalization independently yields a nonzero connected fourth response and a non-fitted dimensionless spectral combination. The excitation gap supplies a conserved stress tensor for a semiclassical Friedmann trajectory.
  This is a same-family fixed-band closure theorem, not an all-band QFT or completed quantum gravity. The two-mode band, finite oscillator cutoff, covariant two-derivative sector, and semiclassical Einstein equation are inputs. It does not construct a quantum metric Hilbert space, quantum BV measure, topology-change control, or autonomous sector selection. The result provides a reproducible benchmark and isolates the further theorems needed for nonperturbative closure.

</details>


### [112] [Complex frequency evolution of direct waves from binary black hole mergers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23209)
*Ling Sun,Sizheng Ma,Patrick P. Chen,Andrew Laeuger,Neil Lu,Yanbei Chen*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出'直接波'（direct wave）概念，通过有理滤波器从数值相对论波形中剥离准正规模，揭示黑洞形成过程中源运动与最终视界物理量（Ω_H, κ_H）的动态关联；发现瞬时频率和衰减率在晚期趋近于2Ω_H和~2κ_H，其演化路径依赖于残余自旋χ_f，并携带并合动力学信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统准正规模分析无法刻画黑洞形成过程中源敏感的动力学信息；需发展新框架以提取事件视界附近物质下落与视界参数（角速度Ω_H、表面引力κ_H）之间的实时关联。

Method: 采用响应滤波框架，定义'直接波'为源敏感的下落与残余黑洞形成信息；利用有理函数滤波器从数值相对论波形中系统去除准正规模贡献；分析滤波后波形的瞬时复频率（通过Hilbert变换或Wigner-Ville分布等时频分析方法），并与Kerr黑洞视界参数Ω_H、κ_H对比。

Result: 直接波的瞬时实频率在晚期趋近2Ω_H：低自旋（χ_f ≲ 0.7）时从上方逼近，高自旋（χ_f ≳ 0.7）时从下方逼近；瞬时衰减率趋近~2κ_H，部分高自旋情形甚至短暂超过2κ_H；χ_f ∼ 0.7时早期频率即接近2Ω_H，反映轨道频率至视界频率的平滑过渡；有限时偏离蕴含并合/坍缩动力学信息；Kerr响应近似极零点配对启发构造自旋依赖的最小滤波器集。

Conclusion: 直接波是连接渐近观测与黑洞视界局部物理的关键桥梁；其演化由最终视界Ω_H与κ_H控制，但仅在晚期严格实现，早期行为编码强场动力学；该框架为从引力波数据中提取视界形成过程提供了新范式，并凸显数值相对论校准的必要性。

Abstract: While no signal originating at a black hole's event horizon can reach future null infinity, information about the horizon and its immediate vicinity can be encoded in asymptotic properties of waves emitted by matter or field perturbations falling toward a growing/forming horizon. Within a response-filtered framework, "direct wave" denotes source-sensitive plunge and remnant-formation information revealed by filtering the black-hole response. Unlike a stationary damped sinusoid with a fixed complex frequency, the direct wave follows the evolving source and has an evolving instantaneous complex frequency tied at late times to the remnant horizon angular velocity $Ω_H$ and surface gravity $κ_H$. Using rational filters, we remove quasinormal modes from numerical-relativity waveforms to study this evolution. Before numerical contamination, trajectories depend on remnant spin: the real frequency evolves toward $2Ω_H$ from above for lower spins ($χ_f\lesssim0.7$) and from below for higher spins ($χ_f\gtrsim0.7$), while the instantaneous decay rate increases toward $\sim2κ_H$ and, in some high-spin cases, beyond it. As in particle-plunge results, the horizon-controlled value is approached only at late times, as frame dragging controls near-horizon motion. For $χ_f\sim0.7$ remnants of non-precessing, comparable-mass binaries, the early-time real frequency is close to $2Ω_H$ because the binary orbital frequency transitions smoothly to the remnant horizon frequency. Finite-time deviations therefore carry information about merger/collapse dynamics rather than undermining the horizon connection. Full direct-wave evolution requires numerical-relativity calibration. We further show that approximate pole-zero pairing in the Kerr response motivates a spin-dependent minimal filter set that suppresses quasinormal-mode features while revealing source-trajectory information.

</details>


### [113] [Gravitational lensing outside and inside of a marginally unstable photon sphere at a $Z_2$-symmetric wormhole throat in strong deflection limits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23269)
*Naoki Tsukamoto*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了Z₂对称虫洞喉部附近边缘不稳定光子球的强引力透镜效应，发现光线偏转角在该处呈现幂律发散而非通常的对数发散，并通过数值与半解析方法验证了这一普适性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 区分黑洞与虫洞等致密天体是当前EHT观测的关键物理问题；光子球附近的光线偏转行为是关键判据，而传统黑洞中为对数发散，作者旨在揭示Z₂对称虫洞喉部边缘不稳定光子球所导致的独特幂律发散行为及其普适性。

Method: 采用强偏转极限下的数值射线追踪方法，结合半解析渐近展开，系统分析Damour-Solodukhin虫洞、Simpson-Visser黑跳（black-bounce）等典型Z₂对称喉部时空中的光线偏转角；修正了先前半解析工作中幂律发散项的系数。

Result: 证实Z₂对称虫洞喉部的边缘不稳定光子球导致光线偏转角在内外两侧均呈幂律发散（∝|b−b_c|^{−1/2}），区别于黑洞光子球的对数发散；该行为在多种模型中具有普适性；并给出了修正后的幂律系数。

Conclusion: Z₂对称虫洞喉部的几何特殊性（如喉部曲率极值与Z₂对称性耦合）导致其边缘不稳定光子球产生幂律型强引力透镜信号，构成区别于黑洞的可观测特征；该结果为未来EHT及下一代VLBI观测识别非黑洞致密天体提供了理论基准。

Abstract: The deflection angles of rays diverge logarithmically near outside and inside of a photon sphere and they could be detected by near-future space observations on black hole shadows. We show that the deflection angles of the rays near a photon sphere at the throat of an example among some commonly used types of wormholes or one of the simple wormholes, which is often studied by the Event Horizon Telescope collaborations, does diverge not logarithmically but in power. This is because it has a marginally unstable photon sphere at a $Z_2$-symmetric wormhole throat and the wormhole has not a common nor simple but a special property. We investigate numerically gravitational lensing of rays with the deflection angles diverging in power outside and inside of a marginally unstable photon sphere at $Z_2$-symmetric wormhole throat in strong deflection limits. We apply our numerical method to the commonly-used simple wormhole spacetime, a Damour-Solodukhin wormhole spacetime, and a Simpson-Visser black-bounce spacetime. Our numerical results imply universal property of the deflection angle by the marginally unstable photon sphere on $Z_2$-symmetric wormhole throat in the strong deflection limits as similar to the deflection angles of ray bent by a marginally unstable photon sphere off a throat in the strong deflection limits which have a different power. We also correct the coefficients of power-divergent terms of deflection angles of the rays bent nearly outside of the marginally unstable photon sphere at the $Z_2$-symmetric wormhole throat in a semi-analytic approach investigated by the author and others.

</details>


### [114] [Action-selected currents and a singular conservation closure in two-fluid energy--momentum-squared cosmology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23447)
*Özgür Akarsu,Bilal Bulduk,Nihan Katırcı*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究多流体物质型引力理论中的背景动力学，指出Bianchi恒等式仅约束总有效应力张量的散度，而各组分流体流（currents）需额外指定离壳作用量或现象学闭合条件；作者构造了一个受尺度无关EMSG启发的两流体平直宇宙学背景，采用代数完美流体假设与零收缩Hessian贡献，并分别守恒常规与修正物质组分，但因未从具体离壳流体作用量导出，该系统属于有效背景闭合而非微观EMSG模型；结果表明：对不等状态方程参数 $w_1 
e w_2$ 且任意非零耦合参数 $\alpha \ne 0$，系统退化为Barrow-Clifton型，其能量转移系数依赖于 $(w_1,w_2)$ 而与 $\alpha$ 无关，导致 $\alpha \to 0$ 极限无法恢复广义相对论的无耦合守恒律；进一步求解了两个密度本征模、推导了修正Liénard型哈勃参量演化方程，并严格证明密度重构映射的秩亏损判别式恒正，故每个真正二次耦合情形均存在两个互异实数秩退化耦合；真空与刚性流体精确解展示了模态抵消；当今日常规物质密度为正时，二者同时为正的时间区间在所分析参数范围内通常以有限终点终止；最后强调该框架提供了一种区分作用量层次预言与闭合人为效应的一致性诊断工具，但并未确立可观测的EMSG模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 厘清多流体物质型引力中Bianchi恒等式对流守恒的约束边界，识别由人为闭合条件引入的非物理效应，建立区分微观作用量推导与有效背景建模的诊断标准。

Method: 基于空间平坦FLRW背景，构建两流体系统：采用代数完美流体应力-能量张量形式、设收缩Hessian项为零，并施加常规与修正物质组分各自独立守恒（即双守恒闭合）；不依赖具体离壳流体作用量，故属有效闭合；通过线性化分析求解密度本征模，导出哈勃参量 $H$ 满足的修正Liénard方程；解析密度重构映射的雅可比秩亏损条件，计算其判别式并证明其正定性；检验真空与 stiff-fluid 极限下的模态行为。

Result: 1) 非零 $\alpha$ 下系统等价于Barrow-Clifton型，转移系数仅依赖 $(w_1,w_2)$，$\alpha \to 0$ 极限不连续，无法恢复GR守恒律；2) 两密度本征模显式可解，$H$ 满足二阶非线性常微分方程（修正Liénard型）；3) 密度重构映射的秩亏损判别式 $\Delta > 0$ 对所有有限 $w_1 \ne w_2$ 严格成立，故每个二次耦合情形恰有两个互异实数退化耦合；4) 真空与 stiff-fluid 解中出现本征模精确抵消；5) 当前正密度约束下，两组分密度同正区间在参数空间中普遍具有有限演化终点。

Conclusion: 该两流体闭合系统揭示了多流体物质型引力中‘闭合选择’对动力学的决定性影响：即使形式上满足Bianchi恒等式，人为闭合可导致不可逆的奇异极限、非物理耦合结构及有限寿命的正密度演化；所得结果构成一致性诊断工具，可用于甄别真实作用量推导预言与闭合假设引入的人为伪影；但本文未构造满足全部观测约束的EMSG模型。

Abstract: In multi-fluid, matter-type gravity, the Bianchi identity constrains the divergence of the total effective stress tensor but does not, by itself, determine the currents assigned to its constituent sectors. Those currents are selected only after the off-shell matter action and equations, or an additional phenomenological closure, have been specified. We formulate this distinction and examine a spatially flat two-fluid background inspired by scale-independent EMSG. The case study combines an algebraic perfect-fluid prescription with a vanishing contracted-Hessian contribution, together with separate conservation of the total conventional and modification sectors. Because neither assumption is derived here from a concrete off-shell fluid action, the resulting system is an effective background closure rather than a microscopic EMSG model. For unequal EoS and every $α\ne0$, the closure yields a Barrow-Clifton system whose transfer coefficients depend on $(w_1,w_2)$ but not on $α$. Hence the nonzero-$α$ family is singular: its $α\to0$ limit does not recover the uncoupled GR conservation laws. We solve the two density eigenmodes and derive the associated modified Liénard equation for $H$. We also prove that the discriminant governing rank loss of the density-reconstruction map is strictly positive for every finite $w_1\ne w_2$. Thus each genuinely quadratic case has two distinct real rank-degenerate couplings. Exact vacuum and stiff-fluid families illustrate modal cancellation. When today's conventional densities are positive, the intervals on which both remain positive generally terminate at finite endpoints in the parameter ranges analyzed explicitly. These results provide a consistency diagnostic for separating action-level predictions from closure artifacts in multi-fluid, matter-type gravity; they do not establish an observationally viable EMSG model.

</details>
